This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   I am the independent scientific Reviewer for the `contamcal-irt-calibration` project. My role is to independently verify experiment conclusions, audit GPU resource compliance (cuda:0-5 boundary), check registry accuracy, and report findings to the Director via `reviewer_post_to_director`. This session continued from a compacted conversation where Reviews #24-28 had been posted. All output must be in Chinese. I am read-only — I do not execute experiments or modify files. The primary ongoing task is monitoring 6 concurrent GPU processes, verifying training completions, and ensuring the Agent properly handles the SFT→eval pipeline.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **4PL+γ IRT model**: Item Response Theory with contamination parameter γ, fitted via Stan NUTS MCMC
   - **Multi-seed SFT strategy**: Training with seed=123 in addition to seed=42 to increase N_models for IRT statistical power
   - **D020 conservative params**: lr=5e-5, alpha=16 for 3B models to fix catastrophic forgetting (vs original lr=2e-4, alpha=32)
   - **Per-scale ground truth (#18 fix)**: `compute_ground_truth` uses same-scale d0 models as counterfactual reference
   - **D018 exclusion**: 3B GSM8K excluded from dose-response analysis due to catastrophic forgetting
   - **Signal pipeline**: embedding, minkpp, selfcritique signals computed per model×benchmark (12 tasks for 1.5B: 4 dosages × 3 benchmarks)
   - **Delta exposure (D024)**: Subtracting d000 baseline signal per-scale
   - **GSM8K generation eval**: Uses CoT generation (~7.5s/item for 3B) unlike MMLU MC format (~80 it/s for 0.5B)
   - **Pooled IRT**: Only valid when all scales have same-source signal-derived exposure
   - **Target**: EMNLP 2026 ARR May 25 (10 days from May 15)
   - **Project GPU allocation**: 6× RTX 4090D (cuda:0-5) on server cqa1-21977

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Key findings:

1. **0.5B d100 s123 eval**: 
   - MMLU completed: **accuracy = 0.3058 (4294/14042)**
   - GSM8K generation started at 05:33:52, currently at 15/1319 = 1.1%, ~5.04 s/it
   - ETA for GSM8K: ~1319 × 5.04s ≈ 6648s ≈ 110 min ≈ 1.8h from start
   - GSM8K should finish around 07:25 UTC

That MMLU score of 0.3058 seems surprisingly low for a 0.5B model—the baseline Qwen2.5-0.5B typically scores around 0.45-0.47, so even with the d100 contamination dosage, I'd expect better performance unless the SFT training degraded the model's general capabilities.

The score is barely above random guessing on a 4-option multiple choice test, which makes me wonder if there's either a serious issue with how the model learned from the contaminated data, or possibly a problem with the evaluation setup itself—maybe a formatting or tokenization mismatch. The log filename mentions "aligned" evaluation, which might indicate a specific evaluation protocol I should consider, but regardless, this result is concerning enough that I need to investigate what went wrong with the 0.5B d100 s123 model.

I should look at the clean baseline results for comparison to see if this is a consistent pattern with contamination, or if something else is going on. I also want to check what's happening with the other GPU runs and SFT experiments to get a fuller picture.

The 1.5B signals pipeline is progressing through MMLU evaluation and should wrap up around 05:55-06:00 UTC, while the 0.5B eval is generating GSM8K results on GPU 1, and the 1.5B d100 model is still training on GPU 2.

Now I'm noticing the 0.5B MMLU score of 0.3058 seems low, so I want to check how this compares against other 0.5B model evaluations in the registry to understand if something's off with this particular run.
```
